# Noise check — supplementary, not pre-specified

Margins of RQ2 and RQ3 (one row per question, comparison, dataset and unit). `n`: seeds behind a margin; the pre-specified estimator uses the n − 1 differences of neighbouring seeds. Zero: every difference is zero (a discrete or a constant metric), the margin is undefined. Near zero: above zero and below 1% of the median margin of the unit over datasets.
